# Gap table: plant

Made by `tools/views/gaps.py`. The 1200 species most recorded worldwide that borrow this view cover 50.5% of its 44,079,457 research-grade records; the 20 most recorded in Alachua County cover 23.1% of the county's 56,850. Counts are iNaturalist research-grade records through 2024. The view's own measured animal is not excluded: it shows as `m` where it was measured.

Row grades: `m` every trait of the row measured in that species; `g` every trait measured or carried by a rule; `i` at least one trait neither. The detail columns give the state of each trait.

Rows and their traits:

- **No picture**: eye_type
- **Neighbours**: sense_light_nonvisual, sense_chemical
- **Green and time**: sense_light_nonvisual, diel
